This study contributes to the bounded rationality literature by positioning GAI as a cognitive scaffold that can detect and resolve ambiguities managers might overlook, but whose own artificial limitations necessitate human management to ensure its reliability as a strategic partner.

</details>


### [80] [Phi-4-reasoning-vision-15B Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03975)
*Jyoti Aneja,Michael Harrison,Neel Joshi,Tyler LaBonte,John Langford,Eduardo Salinas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了 Phi-4-reasoning-vision-15B 模型，揭示了如何通过极致的数据精炼、动态编码器架构和混合推理模式，打造出一个在科学数学推理和 UI 理解上极具竞争力的小型开源多模态模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在探索如何通过高效的设计和高质量的数据，构建出体量较小、计算成本低但具备强大科学/数学推理及 UI 理解能力的多模态模型，并为开源社区提供实践经验。

Method: 采用了高分辨率动态编码器（Dynamic-resolution encoders），实施了系统的多模态数据过滤、错误修正和合成增强，并引入显式模式令牌（Mode tokens）来区分直接回答与思维链推理（CoT）。

Result: 该 15B 模型在通用视听任务上表现优异，并在科学、数学推理及用户界面理解方面表现卓越；实验表明数据质量（而非规模）是提升性能的关键，动态分辨率能显著增强感知精度。

Conclusion: Phi-4-reasoning-vision-15B 证明了通过精心的数据管理、架构优化和任务模式引导，可以在较小的模型参数下实现超越规模的高性能多模态推理能力。

Abstract: We present Phi-4-reasoning-vision-15B, a compact open-weight multimodal reasoning model, and share the motivations, design choices, experiments, and learnings that informed its development. Our goal is to contribute practical insight to the research community on building smaller, efficient multimodal reasoning models and to share the result of these learnings as an open-weight model that is good at common vision and language tasks and excels at scientific and mathematical reasoning and understanding user interfaces. Our contributions include demonstrating that careful architecture choices and rigorous data curation enable smaller, open-weight multimodal models to achieve competitive performance with significantly less training and inference-time compute and tokens. The most substantial improvements come from systematic filtering, error correction, and synthetic augmentation -- reinforcing that data quality remains the primary lever for model performance. Systematic ablations show that high-resolution, dynamic-resolution encoders yield consistent improvements, as accurate perception is a prerequisite for high-quality reasoning. Finally, a hybrid mix of reasoning and non-reasoning data with explicit mode tokens allows a single model to deliver fast direct answers for simpler tasks and chain-of-thought reasoning for complex problems.

</details>


### [81] [BeamPERL: Parameter-Efficient RL with Verifiable Rewards Specializes Compact LLMs for Structured Beam Mechanics Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04124)
*Tarjei Paule Hage,Markus J. Buehler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现，虽然强化学习能显著提升语言模型解物理题的准确率，但模型倾向于学习解题模板而非物理定律，导致其在面对拓扑结构变化时缺乏泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究带有硬性、可验证奖励的强化学习是否能教会小型语言模型物理推理能力，还是仅仅让其学会了针对正确答案的模式匹配（Pattern-matching）。

Method: 使用 1.5B 参数的模型，在经典工程问题“梁静力学”上进行实验。采用基于符号求解器提供的二进制正确性奖励（RLVR），在没有教师生成推理轨迹的情况下进行参数高效的强化学习。

Result: 模型在 Pass@1 指标上提升了 66.7%。然而，这种能力是各向异性的：模型在组合性变换（增加载荷）上表现良好，但在拓扑结构变化（移动支座）下失败。此外，过度训练会导致模型在维持高奖励的同时丧失鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 仅靠精确的奖励信号不足以确保模型内化物理定律。为了实现鲁棒的科学推理，验证性奖励必须与结构化的推理支架（scaffolding）相结合，以超越模板匹配阶段。

Abstract: Can reinforcement learning with hard, verifiable rewards teach a compact language model to reason about physics, or does it primarily learn to pattern-match toward correct answers? We study this question by training a 1.5B-parameter reasoning model on beam statics, a classic engineering problem, using parameter-efficient RLVR with binary correctness rewards from symbolic solvers, without teacher-generated reasoning traces. The best BeamPERL checkpoint achieves a 66.7% improvement in Pass@1 over the base model. However, the learned competence is anisotropic: the model generalizes compositionally (more loads) but fails under topological shifts (moved supports) that require the same equilibrium equations. Intermediate checkpoints yield the strongest reasoning, while continued optimization degrades robustness while maintaining reward. These findings reveal a key limitation of outcome-level alignment: reinforcement learning with exact physics rewards induces procedural solution templates rather than internalization of governing equations. The precision of the reward signal - even when analytically exact - does not by itself guarantee transferable physical reasoning. Our results suggest that verifiable rewards may need to be paired with structured reasoning scaffolding to move beyond template matching toward robust scientific reasoning.

</details>


### [82] [Towards Realistic Personalization: Evaluating Long-Horizon Preference Following in Personalized User-LLM Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04191)
*Qianyun Guo,Yibo Li,Yue Liu,Bryan Hooi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 RealPref 基准，用于评估 LLM 在长文本和不同表达方式下遵循个性化偏好的能力，揭示了当前模型在处理隐式偏好和长上下文方面的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估大语言模型在长期、真实的个性化交互中遵循复杂多样用户偏好的能力。

Method: 构建了包含 100 个用户个人资料、1300 个偏好设置、4 种偏好表达方式（显式到隐式）及长交互历史的基准测试，并配套三种题型及详细的评测准则。

Result: 模型性能随着上下文长度增加和偏好表达变得隐式而显著下降，且在将偏好理解泛化到未见场景时面临重大挑战。

Conclusion: RealPref 为开发具备用户感知能力的 LLM 助手奠定了基础，并指出提升长文本理解及隐式偏好捕捉能力是未来的关键方向。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly serving as personal assistants, where users share complex and diverse preferences over extended interactions. However, assessing how well LLMs can follow these preferences in realistic, long-term situations remains underexplored. This work proposes RealPref, a benchmark for evaluating realistic preference-following in personalized user-LLM interactions. RealPref features 100 user profiles, 1300 personalized preferences, four types of preference expression (ranging from explicit to implicit), and long-horizon interaction histories. It includes three types of test questions (multiple-choice, true-or-false, and open-ended), with detailed rubrics for LLM-as-a-judge evaluation. Results indicate that LLM performance significantly drops as context length grows and preference expression becomes more implicit, and that generalizing user preference understanding to unseen scenarios poses further challenges. RealPref and these findings provide a foundation for future research to develop user-aware LLM assistants that better adapt to individual needs. The code is available at https://github.com/GG14127/RealPref.

</details>


### [83] [Agentics 2.0: Logical Transduction Algebra for Agentic Data Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04241)
*Alfio Massimiliano Gliozzo,Junkyu Lee,Nahuel Defosse*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文推出 Agentics 2.0，这是一个轻量级 Python 原生框架，通过逻辑转导代数实现强类型、可溯源且高度并行的 Agent 任务流，显著提升了大型语言模型处理复杂数据任务的可靠性与性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着 Agentic AI 从原型研究转向企业部署，现有的方案难以满足软件工程对可靠性、可扩展性和可观测性的高要求，且往往缺乏结构化的数据流管理。

Method: 提出核心概念“逻辑转导代数（Logical Transduction Algebra）”，将 LLM 推理建模为有类型的语义转换（Transducible Function）。该框架支持强类型模式验证、证据溯源，并通过无状态异步 Map-Reduce 算子实现并行化执行。

Result: 在 DiscoveryBench（数据驱动发现）和 Archer（NL-to-SQL 语义解析）等挑战性基准测试中，Agentics 2.0 均取得了当前最先进（SOTA）的性能表现。

Conclusion: Agentics 2.0 为企业级 Agentic AI 提供了兼具理论严密性与工程实用性的框架，推动了从“文本生成”向“结构化数据处理”的范式转变。

Abstract: Agentic AI is rapidly transitioning from research prototypes to enterprise deployments, where requirements extend to meet the software quality attributes of reliability, scalability, and observability beyond plausible text generation. We present Agentics 2.0, a lightweight, Python-native framework for building high-quality, structured, explainable, and type-safe agentic data workflows. At the core of Agentics 2.0, the logical transduction algebra formalizes a large language model inference call as a typed semantic transformation, which we call a transducible function that enforces schema validity and the locality of evidence. The transducible functions compose into larger programs via algebraically grounded operators and execute as stateless asynchronous calls in parallel in asynchronous Map-Reduce programs. The proposed framework provides semantic reliability through strong typing, semantic observability through evidence tracing between slots of the input and output types, and scalability through stateless parallel execution. We instantiate reusable design patterns and evaluate the programs in Agentics 2.0 on challenging benchmarks, including DiscoveryBench for data-driven discovery and Archer for NL-to-SQL semantic parsing, demonstrating state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [84] [$τ$-Knowledge: Evaluating Conversational Agents over Unstructured Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04370)
*Quan Shi,Alexandra Zytek,Pedram Razavi,Karthik Narasimhan,Victor Barres*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文推出 $\tau$-Knowledge 基准，专门评估智能体在真实金融场景中结合大规模非结构化知识与工具操作的能力，实验显示现有模型在此类复杂任务中表现依然疲软。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基准测试通常将检索和工具使用分开评估，缺乏在非结构化数据和长程交互环境下对智能体协同能力、政策合规性的综合评估。

Method: 扩展了 $\tau$-Bench 框架，引入 $\tau$-Banking 领域，涵盖约 700 个互联的知识文档和工具调用接口。通过嵌入式检索和终端搜索两种方式，评估智能体在长对话流程中的表现。

Result: 即便是拥有高推理预算的最先进模型（Frontier Models），在该基准上的通过率也仅约为 25.5%，且可靠性随实验次数增加而大幅下降，主要难点在于从高密度关联的知识库中检索正确文档以及理解复杂政策。

Conclusion: 当前的大语言模型在处理知识密集且具有严格政策限制的任务时表现不佳，$\tau$-Knowledge 为开发更可靠的知识型智能体提供了一个具有挑战性的基准。

Abstract: Conversational agents are increasingly deployed in knowledge-intensive settings, where correct behavior depends on retrieving and applying domain-specific knowledge from large, proprietary, and unstructured corpora during live interactions with users. Yet most existing benchmarks evaluate retrieval or tool use independently of each other, creating a gap in realistic, fully agentic evaluation over unstructured data in long-horizon interactions. We introduce $τ$-Knowledge, an extension of $τ$-Bench for evaluating agents in environments where success depends on coordinating external, natural-language knowledge with tool outputs to produce verifiable, policy-compliant state changes. Our new domain, $τ$-Banking, models realistic fintech customer support workflows in which agents must navigate roughly 700 interconnected knowledge documents while executing tool-mediated account updates. Across embedding-based retrieval and terminal-based search, even frontier models with high reasoning budgets achieve only $\sim$25.5% pass^1, with reliability degrading sharply over repeated trials. Agents struggle to retrieve the correct documents from densely interlinked knowledge bases and to reason accurately over complex internal policies. Overall, $τ$-Knowledge provides a realistic testbed for developing agents that integrate unstructured knowledge in human-facing deployments.

</details>


### [85] [A Dual-Helix Governance Approach Towards Reliable Agentic AI for WebGIS Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04390)
*Boyuan,Guan,Wencong Cui,Levente Juhasz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对 WebGIS 开发中 LLM 的不可靠性，本文提出了 AgentLoom 治理框架，通过三轨架构和知识图谱将 AI 行为规范化，显著提升了大型 WebGIS 代码的可维护性与重构效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WebGIS 开发需要极高的严谨性，但现有的智能体 AI 常因 LLM 的五大局限（上下文约束、跨会话遗忘、随机性、指令失效、适配僵化）而导致项目失败。

Method: 提出一种“双螺旋治理框架”，采用知识（Knowledge）、行为（Behavior）、技能（Skills）三轨架构。通过知识图谱外化领域事实并强制执行协议，并结合自学习循环实现知识增长。

Result: 在 FutureShorelines 项目中，受治理的智能体将 2265 行单体代码重构为模块化组件，使圈复杂度降低 51%，维护性指数提高 7 个百分点。对比实验证明其可靠性优于零样本（zero-shot）LLM。

Conclusion: 在地理空间工程中，仅依靠模型能力是不够的，外部化的治理（governance）是驱动操作可靠性的关键。该研究为生成式 AI 在 WebGIS 及更广泛的软件工程应用中提供了新范式。

Abstract: WebGIS development requires rigor, yet agentic AI frequently fails due to five large language model (LLM) limitations: context constraints, cross-session forgetting, stochasticity, instruction failure, and adaptation rigidity. We propose a dual-helix governance framework reframing these challenges as structural governance problems that model capacity alone cannot resolve. We implement the framework as a 3-track architecture (Knowledge, Behavior, Skills) that uses a knowledge graph substrate to stabilize execution by externalizing domain facts and enforcing executable protocols, complemented by a self-learning cycle for autonomous knowledge growth. Applying this to the FutureShorelines WebGIS tool, a governed agent refactored a 2,265-line monolithic codebase into modular ES6 components. Results demonstrated a 51\% reduction in cyclomatic complexity and a 7-point increase in maintainability index. A comparative experiment against a zero-shot LLM confirms that externalized governance, not just model capability, drives operational reliability in geospatial engineering. This approach is implemented in the open-source AgentLoom governance toolkit.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [86] [The Theory behind UMAP?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03375)
*David Wegmann*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文指出了流行维数约减算法UMAP在理论基础上的数学推导错误，并提供了修复后的完整范畴论推导与算法属性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UMAP算法的核心理论源自Spivak一份未发表且包含大量错误的草稿，而McInnes等人在后续文献中延续了这些错误，导致UMAP的理论证明存在缺陷。

Method: 文章对Spivak的度量实现（metric realization）及其范畴论演算进行了修正，提供了所有函子的完整推导，并专门针对McInnes等人的有限变体（finite variant）进行了重新表述。

Result: 作者提供了一个自包含的、显式的度量实现及相关函子的数学描述，并批判性地讨论了UMAP算法的实际属性与其理论宣称之间的对应关系。

Conclusion: 本研究旨在纠正UMAP算法理论基础中的广泛错误，为该流行维数约减算法及其背后范畴论工具的后续研究建立正确的数学基础。

Abstract: In 2018, McInnes et al. introduced a dimensionality reduction algorithm called UMAP, which enjoys wide popularity among data scientists. Their work introduces a finite variant of a functor called the metric realization, based on an unpublished draft by Spivak. This draft contains many errors, most of which are reproduced by McInnes et al. and subsequent publications. This article aims to repair these errors and provide a self-contained document with the full derivation of Spivak's functors and McInnes et al.'s finite variant. We contribute an explicit description of the metric realization and related functors. At the end, we discuss the UMAP algorithm, as well as claims about properties of the algorithm and the correspondence of McInnes et al.'s finite variant to the UMAP algorithm.

</details>


### [87] [Learning Order Forest for Qualitative-Attribute Data Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03387)
*Mingjie Zhao,Sen Feng,Yiqun Zhang,Mengke Li,Yang Lu,Yiu-ming Cheung*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种通过构建“森林”结构来表征定性属性间距离的聚类方法，通过联合学习树结构与聚类过程，显著提升了定性数据的聚类准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的欧氏距离不适用于具有定性属性（如症状、婚姻状况）的数据，因为这些属性值之间缺乏直观的距离定义和顺序关系。

Method: 提出一种联合学习机制，将定性属性值建模为树结构中的顶点，并在聚类过程中迭代优化树结构（森林）和聚类结果。

Result: 在12个真实基准数据集上与10种主流方法进行对比，实验结果和显著性检验均证明了该方法在定性数据聚类上的优越性。

Conclusion: 该方法通过联合学习机制有效地学习了定性属性间的层级距离结构，能够更好地刻画定性数据的潜在分布。

Abstract: Clustering is a fundamental approach to understanding data patterns, wherein the intuitive Euclidean distance space is commonly adopted. However, this is not the case for implicit cluster distributions reflected by qualitative attribute values, e.g., the nominal values of attributes like symptoms, marital status, etc. This paper, therefore, discovered a tree-like distance structure to flexibly represent the local order relationship among intra-attribute qualitative values. That is, treating a value as the vertex of the tree allows to capture rich order relationships among the vertex value and the others. To obtain the trees in a clustering-friendly form, a joint learning mechanism is proposed to iteratively obtain more appropriate tree structures and clusters. It turns out that the latent distance space of the whole dataset can be well-represented by a forest consisting of the learned trees. Extensive experiments demonstrate that the joint learning adapts the forest to the clustering task to yield accurate results. Comparisons of 10 counterparts on 12 real benchmark datasets with significance tests verify the superiority of the proposed method.

</details>


### [88] [Beyond Cross-Validation: Adaptive Parameter Selection for Kernel-Based Gradient Descents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03401)
*Xiaotong Liu,Yunwen Lei,Xiangyu Chang,Shao-Bo Lin*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于偏置-方差分析的 KGD 算法自适应参数选择策略，理论证明其在多种任务下均能达到最优泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在为基于核的梯度下降（KGD）算法提出一种更有效、可实施的参数选择方案。

Method: 结合偏置-方差分析与分裂法（splitting method），引入“经验有效维度”来量化迭代增量，从而导出可执行的自适应参数选择策略。

Result: 在学习理论框架下，利用积分算子法证明了该 KGD 策略能有效适应不同的核、目标函数及误差指标。

Conclusion: 该策略在泛化误差界上达到了最优，并展现出优于现有方法的适应性。

Abstract: This paper proposes a novel parameter selection strategy for kernel-based gradient descent (KGD) algorithms, integrating bias-variance analysis with the splitting method. We introduce the concept of empirical effective dimension to quantify iteration increments in KGD, deriving an adaptive parameter selection strategy that is implementable. Theoretical verifications are provided within the framework of learning theory. Utilizing the recently developed integral operator approach, we rigorously demonstrate that KGD, equipped with the proposed adaptive parameter selection strategy, achieves the optimal generalization error bound and adapts effectively to different kernels, target functions, and error metrics. Consequently, this strategy showcases significant advantages over existing parameter selection methods for KGD.

</details>


### [89] [Surprisal-Rényi Free Energy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03405)
*Shion Matsumoto,Raul Castillo,Benjamin Prada,Ankur Arjun Mali*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新型泛函 SRFE，通过引入似然比的对数方差和变分特征，统一并解释了前向与后向 KL 散度在学习推理中的归纳偏置差异及其大偏差结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前向和后向 KL 散度在诱导归纳偏置方面存在显著差异，且这种差异无法仅通过期望层面来解释。作者旨在寻找一种能够连接并解释这两者极限动态的理论框架。

Method: 引入了惊异-瑞尼自由能（SRFE），这是一种基于似然比对数矩的泛函，不属于传统的 $f$-散度类。通过对 SRFE 进行局部展开、吉布斯型变分特征提取以及基于 Chernoff 界的理论推导进行分析。

Result: 1. SRFE 在端点极限下可还原为前向和后向 KL 散度；2. 局部展开显示似然比对数方差是衡量偏离 KL 机制的一阶修正项；3. 确立了 SRFE 作为 KL 散度加权和的唯一极小值特性；4. 证明了 SRFE 能通过 Chernoff 界直接控制超额码长的概率大偏差。

Conclusion: SRFE 被确认为一种对方差和尾部敏感的自由能泛函，能够在不强行统一不同学习框架的前提下，揭示前向/后向 KL 散度极限背后的几何与大偏差结构。

Abstract: The forward and reverse Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergences arise as limiting objectives in learning and inference yet induce markedly different inductive biases that cannot be explained at the level of expectations alone. In this work, we introduce the Surprisal-Rényi Free Energy (SRFE), a log-moment-based functional of the likelihood ratio that lies outside the class of $f$-divergences. We show that SRFE recovers forward and reverse KL divergences as singular endpoint limits and derive local expansions around both limits in which the variance of the log-likelihood ratio appears as a first-order correction. This reveals an explicit mean-variance tradeoff governing departures from KL-dominated regimes. We further establish a Gibbs-type variational characterization of SRFE as the unique minimizer of a weighted sum of KL divergences and prove that SRFE directly controls large deviations of excess code-length via Chernoff-type bounds, yielding a precise Minimum Description Length interpretation. Together, these results identify SRFE as a variance- and tail-sensitive free-energy functional that clarifies the geometric and large-deviation structure underlying forward and reverse KL limits, without unifying or subsuming distinct learning frameworks.

</details>


### [90] [Scalable Contrastive Causal Discovery under Unknown Soft Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03411)
*Mingxuan Zhang,Khushi Desai,Sopho Kevlishvili,Elham Azizi*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对单一软干预场景的对比式因果发现模型，通过聚合子集PDAG和跨机制定向规则，实现了比传统方法更精准、高可扩展的因果结构识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 观测数据的因果发现仅能识别到马尔可夫等价类，虽然干预可以减少歧义，但实际中的干预往往是具有多个未知目标的“软干预”，且通常只能观测到单一的干预状态。

Method: 提出一种可扩展的因果发现模型，通过聚合子集层面的部分有向无环图（PDAGs），并应用对比跨机制定向规则（contrastive cross-regime orientation rules），在Meek闭包下构建全局一致的最大PDAG。

Result: 理论证明了该模型在受限子集设定下的完备性，并展示了其渐近恢复可识别PDAG的能力；相比于非对比方法，该模型能定向更多的边。

Conclusion: 该模型在合成数据实验中表现出更强的因果结构恢复能力、对未见图结构的泛化性以及在大规模图上的可扩展性。

Abstract: Observational causal discovery is only identifiable up to the Markov equivalence class. While interventions can reduce this ambiguity, in practice interventions are often soft with multiple unknown targets. In many realistic scenarios, only a single intervention regime is observed. We propose a scalable causal discovery model for paired observational and interventional settings with shared underlying causal structure and unknown soft interventions. The model aggregates subset-level PDAGs and applies contrastive cross-regime orientation rules to construct a globally consistent maximal PDAG under Meek closure, enabling generalization to both in-distribution and out-of-distribution settings. Theoretically, we prove that our model is sound with respect to a restricted $Ψ$ equivalence class induced solely by the information available in the subset-restricted setting. We further show that the model asymptotically recovers the corresponding identifiable PDAG and can orient additional edges compared to non-contrastive subset-restricted methods. Experiments on synthetic data demonstrate improved causal structure recovery, generalization to unseen graphs with held-out causal mechanisms, and scalability to larger graphs, with ablations supporting the theoretical results.

</details>


### [91] [Empirical Evaluation of No Free Lunch Violations in Permutation-Based Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03613)
*Grzegorz Sroka*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文通过代数重组基准函数，证明了目标函数的表述方式可以打破“没有免费午餐”定理的性能对称性，从而产生结构性的算法表现差异，强调了算法选择需结合问题表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨在何种情况下，“没有免费午餐（NFL）”定理的平均性能保障不再能反映基准测试的实际结果，即研究目标函数的重新表述和基准设计如何导致局部偏离NFL直觉。

Method: 在无放回迭代搜索设置下，使用二进制目标函数进行穷举评估。通过对基准函数进行代数重组（求和与差值）构建新基准，并利用相关性分析、层次聚类、Delta热图、PCA及带Tukey对比的单因素方差分析进行性能评估。

Result: 均匀采样的基准保持了NFL的全局对称性，但代数修饰后的基准导致了稳定的算法重排名和功能聚类。复合目标表现出非加性的搜索难度。蒙特卡洛实验表明，顺序效应在大空间中依然存在且依赖于函数类别。

Conclusion: 算法选择应同时考虑问题类别和目标的表达形式；这一结论不仅适用于演化计算，也适用于基于重标记、重采样和置换检验的统计程序。

Abstract: The No Free Lunch (NFL) theorem guarantees equal average performance only under uniform sampling of a function space closed under permutation (c.u.p.). We ask when this averaging ceases to reflect what benchmarking actually reports. We study an iterative-search setting with sampling without replacement, where algorithms differ only in evaluation order. Binary objectives allow exhaustive evaluation in the fully enumerable case, and efficiency is defined by the first time the global minimum is reached. We then construct two additional benchmarks by algebraically recombining the same baseline functions through sums and differences. Function-algorithm relations are examined via correlation structure, hierarchical clustering, delta heatmaps, and PCA. A one-way ANOVA with Tukey contrasts confirms that algebraic reformulations induce statistically meaningful shifts in performance patterns. The uniformly sampled baseline remains consistent with the global NFL symmetry. In contrast, the algebraically modified benchmarks yield stable re-rankings and coherent clusters of functions and sampling policies. Composite objectives can also exhibit non-additive search effort despite being built from simpler components. Monte Carlo experiments indicate that order effects persist in larger spaces and depend on function class. Taken together, the results show how objective reformulation and benchmark design can generate structured local departures from NFL intuition. They motivate algorithm choice that is aware of both the problem class and the objective representation. This message applies to evolutionary computation as well as to statistical procedures based on relabeling, resampling, and permutation tests.

</details>


### [92] [Riemannian Langevin Dynamics: Strong Convergence of Geometric Euler-Maruyama Scheme](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03626)
*Zhiyuan Zhan,Masashi Sugiyama*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文证明了黎曼流形上几何 EM 离散方案具有 $1/2$ 阶强收敛性，并将其应用于流形采样的误差分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实数据通常具有低维流形结构，这推动了流形扩散模型的发展。然而，流形值 SDE 的数值离散方案（如 EM 方案）在一般设定下的强收敛理论尚不完善。

Method: 研究了一种针对黎曼流形上 SDE 的几何版本 Euler--Maruyama (EM) 方案，并在特定的几何和正则性条件下证明了其强收敛阶。

Result: 证明了流形上几何 EM 方案具有 $1/2$ 阶的强收敛速度，并利用该结果导出了黎曼 Langevin 动力学在流形采样中的 Wasserstein 误差界。

Conclusion: 该研究填补了流形值 SDE 数值分析的理论空白，为流形上的生成模型（如扩散模型和 Langevin 动力学）提供了坚实的数学支撑。

Abstract: Low-dimensional structure in real-world data plays an important role in the success of generative models, which motivates diffusion models defined on intrinsic data manifolds. Such models are driven by stochastic differential equations (SDEs) on manifolds, which raises the need for convergence theory of numerical schemes for manifold-valued SDEs. In Euclidean space, the Euler--Maruyama (EM) scheme achieves strong convergence with order $1/2$, but an analogous result for manifold discretizations is less understood in general settings. In this work, we study a geometric version of the EM scheme for SDEs on Riemannian manifolds and prove strong convergence with order $1/2$ under geometric and regularity conditions. As an application, we obtain a Wasserstein bound for sampling on manifolds via the geometric EM discretization of Riemannian Langevin dynamics.

</details>


### [93] [Observationally Informed Adaptive Causal Experimental Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03785)
*Erdun Gao,Liang Zhang,Jake Fawkes,Aoqi Zuo,Wenqin Liu,Haoxuan Li,Mingming Gong,Dino Sejdinovic*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出 Active Residual Learning 和 R-Design 框架，通过利用有偏差的观测模型作为先验，将实验重点从“从头学习”转向“修正偏差”，从而显著提升实验设计的效率和因果推断的收敛速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管随机对照试验（RCT）是因果推断的金标准，但其资源稀缺且成本高。传统的试验设计往往忽略现有的观测数据（因担心偏见），导致数据获取效率低下。作者旨在探索如何利用有偏差的观测数据作为先验，以减少实验样本需求。

Method: 提出 Active Residual Learning 范式和 R-Design 框架。其核心是 R-EPIG (Residual Expected Predictive Information Gain) 准则，该准则不直接学习目标量，而是利用 RCT 数据来高效估计和修正观测模型中的“残差”（即由于偏见产生的误差）。

Result: 1. 理论上证明了残差对比的估计具有更快的收敛速度，且标准的主动学习方法（如 BALD）在处理非任务相关的冗余不确定性时存在预算浪费。2. 实验证明 R-Design 在合成及半合成基准上显著优于基准方法。

Conclusion: 在实验设计中，修复一个有偏差的观测模型比从零开始学习更加高效。该方法为将大规模观测数据融入严谨的临床试验/实验设计提供了理论支撑和实践框架。

Abstract: Randomized Controlled Trials (RCTs) represent the gold standard for causal inference yet remain a scarce resource. While large-scale observational data is often available, it is utilized only for retrospective fusion, and remains discarded in prospective trial design due to bias concerns. We argue this "tabula rasa" data acquisition strategy is fundamentally inefficient. In this work, we propose Active Residual Learning, a new paradigm that leverages the observational model as a foundational prior. This approach shifts the experimental focus from learning target causal quantities from scratch to efficiently estimating the residuals required to correct observational bias. To operationalize this, we introduce the R-Design framework. Theoretically, we establish two key advantages: (1) a structural efficiency gap, proving that estimating smooth residual contrasts admits strictly faster convergence rates than reconstructing full outcomes; and (2) information efficiency, where we quantify the redundancy in standard parameter-based acquisition (e.g., BALD), demonstrating that such baselines waste budget on task-irrelevant nuisance uncertainty. We propose R-EPIG (Residual Expected Predictive Information Gain), a unified criterion that directly targets the causal estimand, minimizing residual uncertainty for estimation or clarifying decision boundaries for policy. Experiments on synthetic and semi-synthetic benchmarks demonstrate that R-Design significantly outperforms baselines, confirming that repairing a biased model is far more efficient than learning one from scratch.

</details>


### [94] [Invariance-Based Dynamic Regret Minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03843)
*Margherita Lazzaretto,Jonas Peters,Niklas Pfister*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种处理非平稳线性老虎机的新方法，通过分解奖励模型来提取并保持“不变性”，从而在快速变化的环境中实现更高效的学习和更低的决策遗憾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的非平稳线性老虎机算法通常通过丢弃或减小旧数据权重来处理环境变化，但这会导致样本效率低下。实际上，历史数据中往往包含某些在环境变化中保持不变的部分，可以被重新利用。

Method: 提出 ISD-linUCB 算法。该算法假设奖励模型由平稳和非平稳部分组成，利用历史数据识别不变的成分（invariances），并在后续决策中利用这些不变性进行降维。

Result: 理论分析和实验结果均表明，在环境快速变化且有足够历史数据的情况下，该方法能显著降低累计遗憾（Regret）。

Conclusion: 在非平稳线性老虎机问题中，显式学习并利用奖励模型中的“不变性”可以有效降低问题维度，缓解数据过期带来的性能损失。

Abstract: We consider stochastic non-stationary linear bandits where the linear parameter connecting contexts to the reward changes over time. Existing algorithms in this setting localize the policy by gradually discarding or down-weighting past data, effectively shrinking the time horizon over which learning can occur. However, in many settings historical data may still carry partial information about the reward model. We propose to leverage such data while adapting to changes, by assuming the reward model decomposes into stationary and non-stationary components. Based on this assumption, we introduce ISD-linUCB, an algorithm that uses past data to learn invariances in the reward model and subsequently exploits them to improve online performance. We show both theoretically and empirically that leveraging invariance reduces the problem dimensionality, yielding significant regret improvements in fast-changing environments when sufficient historical data is available.

</details>


### [95] [Stable and Steerable Sparse Autoencoders with Weight Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04198)
*Piotr Jedryszek,Oliver M. Crook*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出通过 L2 权重正则化来提升稀疏自编码器（SAEs）的特征稳定性，在不损失可解释性的前提下显著增强了特征在不同训练运行中的一致性及下游控制任务的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏自编码器（SAEs）提取的特征在不同随机种子和训练选择下往往缺乏稳定性，这影响了模型解释的可靠性。

Method: 在 MNIST 和语言模型（Pythia-70M）上研究了 encoder/decoder 权重的 L1/L2 正则化效果，并结合了 Tie 初始化（tied initialization）和单位范数解码器约束。

Result: L2 正则化显著提升了不同随机种子间的特征一致性，使 TopK SAEs 的激活控制（steering）成功率几乎翻倍，并增强了自动可解释性评分与功能控制力之间的相关性。

Conclusion: 在训练中引入 L2 权重正则化并配合特定约束，是提升 SAEs 特征一致性、可解释性与控制力之间对齐度的有效策略。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) are widely used to extract human-interpretable features from neural network activations, but their learned features can vary substantially across random seeds and training choices. To improve stability, we studied weight regularization by adding L1 or L2 penalties on encoder and decoder weights, and evaluate how regularization interacts with common SAE training defaults. On MNIST, we observe that L2 weight regularization produces a core of highly aligned features and, when combined with tied initialization and unit-norm decoder constraints, it dramatically increases cross-seed feature consistency. For TopK SAEs trained on language model activations (Pythia-70M-deduped), adding a small L2 weight penalty increased the fraction of features shared across three random seeds and roughly doubles steering success rates, while leaving the mean of automated interpretability scores essentially unchanged. Finally, in the regularized setting, activation steering success becomes better predicted by auto-interpretability scores, suggesting that regularization can align text-based feature explanations with functional controllability.

</details>


### [96] [Beyond Mixtures and Products for Ensemble Aggregation: A Likelihood Perspective on Generalized Means](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04204)
*Raphaël Razafindralambo,Rémy Sun,Frédéric Precioso,Damien Garreau,Pierre-Alexandre Mattei*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文通过研究广义平均值，为深度集成的聚合策略提供了理论框架，证明了参数 $r$ 在 0 到 1 之间（包括线性和几何池化）是保证性能提升的安全区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度集成（Deep Ensembles）中的概率分布聚合是一个核心问题。目前常用的聚合方式包括线性池化和几何池化，但对于如何选择最优聚合方式以及为什么这些方式有效，缺乏统一的理论指导。

Method: 研究了归一化广义平均（Normalized Generalized Mean）在不同阶数 $r$ 下的性质。通过对数似然（Log-likelihood）的视角，探讨了不同聚合规则在机器学习评估标准下的表现。

Result: 证明了当 $r \in [0, 1]$ 时，聚合分布能确保相对于单个分布的系统性改进；当 $r \notin [0, 1]$ 时，通过反例证明了聚合可能无法提供一致的增益。图像和文本分类的实验验证了这些结论。

Conclusion: $r \in [0, 1]$ 是确保集成模型效果优于单一分布的唯一区间。这为线性池化 ($r=1$) 和几何池化 ($r=0$) 的广泛应用提供了理论支持，并警告了使用此范围之外参数的风险。

Abstract: Density aggregation is a central problem in machine learning, for instance when combining predictions from a Deep Ensemble. The choice of aggregation remains an open question with two commonly proposed approaches being linear pooling (probability averaging) and geometric pooling (logit averaging). In this work, we address this question by studying the normalized generalized mean of order $r \in \mathbb{R} \cup \{-\infty,+\infty\}$ through the lens of log-likelihood, the standard evaluation criterion in machine learning. This provides a unifying aggregation formalism and shows different optimal configurations for different situations. We show that the regime $r \in [0,1]$ is the only range ensuring systematic improvements relative to individual distributions, thereby providing a principled justification for the reliability and widespread practical use of linear ($r=1$) and geometric ($r=0$) pooling. In contrast, we show that aggregation rules with $r \notin [0,1]$ may fail to provide consistent gains with explicit counterexamples. Finally, we corroborate our theoretical findings with empirical evaluations using Deep Ensembles on image and text classification benchmarks.

</details>


### [97] [Semi-Supervised Generative Learning via Latent Space Distribution Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04223)
*Kwong Yu Chong,Long Feng*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出 LSDM 框架，通过两阶段潜空间分布匹配实现高效的半监督生成，并为潜扩散模型（LDM）提供了新的理论一致性解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决条件生成模型在数据稀缺情况下对大量成对数据的依赖问题，并试图为潜空间生成方法提供统一的理论解释。

Method: 框架分为两个阶段：1) 利用成对和非成对数据学习低维潜空间；2) 仅利用少量的成对数据，在该空间内通过最小化 1-Wasserstein 距离进行联合分布匹配。

Result: 理论上证明了非渐近误差界及非成对数据对几何保真度的提升；实证上在图像生成和超分辨率任务中展示了利用非成对数据提高生成质量的能力。

Conclusion: LSDM 不仅在半监督生成的实证任务中表现出色，还为理解潜空间生成模型（如 LDM）提供了统一的统计理论框架。

Abstract: We introduce Latent Space Distribution Matching (LSDM), a novel framework for semi-supervised generative modeling of conditional distributions. LSDM operates in two stages: (i) learning a low-dimensional latent space from both paired and unpaired data, and (ii) performing joint distribution matching in this space via the 1-Wasserstein distance, using only paired data. This two-step approach minimizes an upper bound on the 1-Wasserstein distance between joint distributions, reducing reliance on scarce paired samples while enabling fast one-step generation. Theoretically, we establish non-asymptotic error bounds and demonstrate a key benefit of unpaired data: enhanced geometric fidelity in generated outputs. Furthermore, by extending the scope of its two core steps, LSDM provides a coherent statistical perspective that connects to a broad class of latent-space approaches. Notably, Latent Diffusion Models (LDMs) can be viewed as a variant of LSDM, in which joint distribution matching is achieved indirectly via score matching. Consequently, our results also provide theoretical insights into the consistency of LDMs. Empirical evaluations on real-world image tasks, including class-conditional generation and image super-resolution, demonstrate the effectiveness of LSDM in leveraging unpaired data to enhance generation quality.

</details>


<div id='cs.IT'></div>

# cs.IT [[Back]](#toc)

### [98] [When Relaxation Does Not Help: RLDCs with Small Soundness Yield LDCs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03717)
*Kuan Cheng,Xin Li,Songtao Mao*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文证明了在特定健全性阈值下，放宽局部可解码代码（RLDC）可以转换为标准局部可解码代码（LDC），并将此结论扩展至非线性代码及局部可校正代码（LCC），从而为这些代码提供了更强的下界约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在探讨局部可解码代码（LDCs）及其放宽版本（RLDCs）之间的关系，特别是要厘清在何种查询次数 $q$ 下，RLDCs 优于 LDCs，或两者在参数上是否等效。

Method: 通过推广和强化先前的研究，消除了对“线性代码”的限制，并证明了任何具有特定健全性误差阈值 $s(q)$ 的 $q$ 次查询 RLDC（即使是不完善的完备性或非自适应解码器）都可以导出参数相当的 LDC。该方法也扩展到了局部可校正代码（LCC）。

Result: 证明了较低健全性误差的 $q$ 次查询 RLDC 会退化为 $q$ 次查询 LDC。同时，基于此结论改进了任意 RLDC、RLCC（放宽局部可校正代码）以及紧密度概率可检查证明（PCPPs）的下界。

Conclusion: 并非所有放宽限制的局部解码代码都能超越标准局部分码。当查询次数固定且健全性误差低于特定阈值时，RLDC 与 LDC 之间存在紧密的理论等价性，这为理解局部解码的边界提供了新视角。

Abstract: Locally decodable codes (LDCs) are error correction codes that allow recovery of any single message symbol by probing only a small number of positions from the (possibly corrupted) codeword. Relaxed locally decodable codes (RLDCs) further allow the decoder to output a special failure symbol $\bot$ on a corrupted codeword. While known constructions of RLDCs achieve much better parameters than standard LDCs, it is intriguing to understand the relationship between LDCs and RLDCs. Separation results (i.e., the existence of $q$-query RLDCs that are not $q$-query LDCs) are known for $q=3$ (Gur, Minzer, Weissenberg, and Zheng, arXiv:2512.12960, 2025) and $q \geq 15$ (Grigorescu, Kumar, Manohar, and Mon, arXiv:2511.02633, 2025), while any $2$-query RLDC also gives a $2$-query LDC (Block, Blocki, Cheng, Grigorescu, Li, Zheng, and Zhu, CCC 2023).
  In this work, we generalize and strengthen the main result in Grigorescu, Kumar, Manohar, and Mon (arXiv:2511.02633, 2025), by removing the requirement of linear codes. Specifically, we show that any $q$-query RLDC with soundness error below some threshold $s(q)$ also yields a $q$-query LDC with comparable parameters. This holds even if the RLDC has imperfect completeness but with a non-adaptive decoder. Our results also extend to the setting of locally correctable codes (LCCs) and relaxed locally correctable codes (RLCCs). Using our results, we further derive improved lower bounds for arbitrary RLDCs and RLCCs, as well as probabilistically checkable proofs of proximity (PCPPs).

</details>


### [99] [A New Class of Geometric Analog Error Correction Codes for Crossbar Based In-Memory Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03723)
*Ziyuan Zhu,Changcheng Yuan,Ron M. Roth,Paul H. Siegel,Anxiao Jiang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文针对模拟内存计算中的多离群值误差问题，研究并分析了一种新型几何码的性能特征，填补了现有模拟纠错码在参数灵活性上的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模拟内存计算（电阻交叉开关）面临小扰动与离群值并存的混合噪声。尽管已有一些针对单或多离群值的模拟纠错码，但现有码族覆盖的长度和维度范围有限，难以满足多样化需求。

Method: 对最近提出的一类能够处理多个离群值的几何码（Geometric Codes）进行深入研究，并开发了一种基于几何分析的方法来刻画其 m-高度分布（m-height profiles）。

Result: 通过几何分析成功表征了该几何码族的 m-高度分布，为评估和提升其在复杂噪声环境下的多离群值纠错能力提供了理论基础。

Conclusion: 几何码在处理电阻交叉开关中的多离群值误差方面具有潜力，未来研究可进一步优化其在大规模计算系统中的实际应用性能。

Abstract: Analog error correction codes have been proposed for analog in-memory computing on resistive crossbars, which can accelerate vector-matrix multiplication for machine learning. Unlike traditional communication or storage channels, this setting involves a mixed noise model with small perturbations and outlier errors. A number of analog codes have been proposed for handling a single outlier, and several constructions have also been developed to address multiple outliers. However, the set of available code families remains limited, covering only a narrow range of code lengths and dimensions. In this paper, we study a recently proposed family of geometric codes capable of handling multiple outliers, and develop a geometric analysis that characterizes their m-height profiles.

</details>


### [100] [Linear codes arising from geometrical operation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03793)
*Antonio Jesús Lorite López,Daniel Camazón Portela,Juan Antonio López Ramos*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文通过单纯复形构造线性码，利用拓扑与几何手段实现了对编码参数（如最小距离）的精确分析与优化，并成功构造出多种最优线性码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在建立单纯复形的拓扑属性与线性码基本参数之间的联系，利用几何视角解决编码参数的可控性问题。

Method: 将任意单纯复形（Simplicial Complexes）与有限域 $F_q$ 上的线性码建立关联，利用组合结构和几何特性分析码重，并探讨拓扑操作对编码参数的影响。

Result: 描述了最小距离与复形几何特征的关系，制定了控制编码参数的几何标准，并构造了几类基于 $F_2$ 的最优线性码。

Conclusion: 通过几何与拓扑方法，该研究提供了一种精确控制和操纵线性码参数的新范式，丰富了编码理论与代数拓扑的交叉研究。

Abstract: We construct linear codes over the finite field Fq from arbitrary simplicial complexes, establishing a connection between topological properties and fundamental coding parameters. First, we study the behaviour of the weights of codewords from a geometric point of view, interpreting them in terms of the combinatorial structure of the associated simplicial complex. This approach allows us to describe the minimum distance of the codes in terms of certain geometric features of the complex. Subsequently, we analyse how various topological operations on simplicial complexes affect the classical parameters of the codes. This study leads to the formulation of geometric criteria that make it possible to explicitly control and manipulate these parameters. Finally, as an application of the obtained results, we construct several families of optimal linear codes over F2 using these geometric methods. Thanks to the previously established geometric properties, we can precisely determine the parameters of these families.

</details>


### [101] [Advances in List Decoding of Polynomial Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03841)
*Mrinal Kumar,Noga Ron-Zewi*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本书综述了Reed-Solomon及其相关多项式纠错码在列表译码领域的最新进展，展示了如何通过高效算法处理极高错误率并达到理论性能极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的纠错码无法处理超出理论极限的高错误率，而计算机科学中的许多应用需要这种能力，因此需要研究列表译码（List-decodable codes）。

Method: 通过综述近年来的研究成果，重点介绍在信息论容量限制下，如何利用最优列表大小及近线性甚至亚线性时间算法进行高效译码。

Result: 实现了Reed-Solomon码及其相关多项式码的高效列表译码，达到了信息论容量上限，并开发出了近线性或亚线性时间的快速算法。

Conclusion: 多项式构造的纠错码（尤其是Reed-Solomon码）在满足信息论极限的列表译码方面已取得突破性进展，并具备高效的算法实现。

Abstract: Error-correcting codes are a method for representing data, so that one can recover the original information even if some parts of it were corrupted. The basic idea, which dates back to the revolutionary work of Shannon and Hamming about a century ago, is to encode the data into a redundant form, so that the original information can be decoded from the redundant encoding even in the presence of some noise or corruption. One prominent family of error-correcting codes are Reed-Solomon Codes which encode the data using evaluations of low-degree polynomials. Nearly six decades after they were introduced, Reed-Solomon Codes, as well as some related families of polynomial-based codes, continue to be widely studied, both from a theoretical perspective and from the point of view of applications.
  Besides their obvious use in communication, error-correcting codes such as Reed-Solomon Codes are also useful for various applications in theoretical computer science. These applications often require the ability to cope with many errors, much more than what is possible information-theoretically. List-decodable codes are a special class of error-correcting codes that enable correction from more errors than is traditionally possible by allowing a small list of candidate decodings. These codes have turned out to be extremely useful in various applications across theoretical computer science and coding theory.
  In recent years, there have been significant advances in list decoding of Reed-Solomon Codes and related families of polynomial-based codes. This includes efficient list decoding of such codes up to the information-theoretic capacity, with optimal list-size, and using fast nearly-linear time, and even sublinear-time, algorithms. In this book, we survey these developments.

</details>


### [102] [Joint Gaussian Beam Pattern and Its Optimization for Positioning-Assisted Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03940)
*Yuanbo Liu,Bingcheng Zhu,Shuojin Huang,Han Zhang,Zaichen Zhang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出并分析了位置辅助的波束成形技术，推导了2D和3D场景下最优高斯波束模式的闭式解，显著降低了大规模MIMO系统的CSI估计成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找CSI基波束成形的替代方案，以解决大规模MIMO系统中训练开销大和反馈成本高的问题。

Method: 通过分析联合高斯波束在2D和3D场景下的中断概率，推导出了闭式表达式，并在此基础上推导出最优波束模式的闭式解，同时分析了近似误差的渐进性能。

Result: 数值结果验证了所推导的中断概率公式的准确性，并展示了波束模式优化在提升通信性能方面的有效性。发现2D场景下的最优解与定位误差分布无关，而3D场景则与之相关。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了在无需复杂CSI估计的情况下，通过位置信息辅助的波束成形方案是可行的，并提供了具体的最优设计准则，特别是在3D场景中需考虑定位误差分布的影响。

Abstract: Beamforming is a fundamental technology that not only enhances communication efficiency but also lays the foundation for massive multiple-input multiple-output~(MIMO) systems. However, its reliance on accurate channel state information (CSI) estimation introduces significant training overhead and feedback costs, especially in large-scale antenna systems. In this paper, we investigate positioning-assisted beamforming as a competitive alternative to the CSI-based methods, which circumvents the complicated CSI estimation. In particular, we analyze the outage probability of positioning-assisted systems with joint Gaussian beams and derive its closed-form expressions for both two-dimensional~(2D) and three-dimensional~(3D) scenarios. Based on these results, we also derive closed-form expressions for the optimal joint Gaussian beam pattern. The optimal solution is independent of the positioning error distribution in 2D scenarios but depends on it in 3D cases. Subsequently, the asymptotic performance of the approximation error is analyzed. Numerical results verify the derived outage probability expressions, and show the effectiveness of the beam pattern optimization.

</details>


### [103] [Distributed vs. Centralized Precoding in Cell-Free Systems: Impact of Realistic Per-AP Power Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03948)
*Wei Jiang,Hans D. Schotten*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 由于集中式预编码在满足实际单AP功率约束时性能大幅下降，分布式预编码在无小区大规模MIMO中更具实用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管集中式预编码在理论上性能卓越，但其通常基于总功率约束推导，忽略了实际中每个接入点（AP）的硬件功率限制。

Method: 研究了集中式预编码在应用两种启发式功率控制方法（全局功率缩放和局部归一化）以满足单AP瞬时功率约束时的性能表现。

Result: 在引入单AP瞬时功率约束后，集中式预编码原有的性能优势消失，表现不再显著优于分布式预编码。

Conclusion: 在实际硬件限制（每个接入点功率受限）下，分布式预编码是比集中式预编码更稳健、更具吸引力的选择。

Abstract: In cell-free massive MIMO, centralized precoding is {theoretically known} to {remarkably} outperform its distributed counterparts, albeit {with} high implementation complexity. However, this letter highlights a practical limitation {often overlooked:} {widely used closed-form} centralized {precoders} are typically derived under a sum-power constraint, which often demands unrealistic power allocation that exceeds hardware capabilities. {When two simple heuristics (global power scaling and local normalization) are applied to enforce the per-AP instantaneous power constraint}, the centralized performance superiority disappears, making distributed precoding {a robust option}.

</details>


### [104] [Training-Free Rate-Distortion-Perception Traversal With Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04005)
*Yuhan Wang,Suzhi Bi,Ying-Jun Angela Zhang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于预训练扩散模型的无需训练框架，通过 RCC 模块和新型 ODE 解码器，实现了对率失真感知（RDP）边界的灵活遍历，并提供了理论最优性证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的神经压缩方法通常只能在率失真感知（RDP）曲面的固定点运行，若要针对不同的权衡目标，则需要重新训练模型，缺乏灵活性。

Method: 提出一个无需重新训练的框架，集成了一个反向信道编码（RCC）模块以及一种新型的分数缩放概率流 ODE 解码器，利用预训练扩散模型来遍历 RDP 曲面。

Result: 理论证明该解码器在 AWGN 观测下具有最优失真感知权衡，且框架在高速斯分布下达到最优 RDP 函数。实验结果证实了其在多个数据集上导航 RDP 三元权衡的有效性。

Conclusion: 本研究建立了一个实用且具有理论依据的方案，用于实现自适应、感知敏感的图像压缩，证明了扩散模型在处理复杂率失真感知权衡方面的巨大潜力。

Abstract: The rate-distortion-perception (RDP) tradeoff characterizes the fundamental limits of lossy compression by jointly considering bitrate, reconstruction fidelity, and perceptual quality. While recent neural compression methods have improved perceptual performance, they typically operate at fixed points on the RDP surface, requiring retraining to target different tradeoffs. In this work, we propose a training-free framework that leverages pre-trained diffusion models to traverse the entire RDP surface. Our approach integrates a reverse channel coding (RCC) module with a novel score-scaled probability flow ODE decoder. We theoretically prove that the proposed diffusion decoder is optimal for the distortion-perception tradeoff under AWGN observations and that the overall framework with the RCC module achieves the optimal RDP function in the Gaussian case. Empirical results across multiple datasets demonstrate the framework's flexibility and effectiveness in navigating the ternary RDP tradeoff using pre-trained diffusion models. Our results establish a practical and theoretically grounded approach to adaptive, perception-aware compression.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [105] [Social Norm Reasoning in Multimodal Language Models: An Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03590)
*Oishik Chowdhury,Anushka Debnath,Bastin Tony Roy Savarimuthu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文评估了多模态大模型（MLLMs）在文本和图像场景下的社交规范推理能力，发现GPT-4o表现最优，但所有模型在处理图像和复杂规范时仍面临挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多智能体系统（NorMAS）中的规范推理多依赖符号化方法，难以应对复杂环境。虽然MLLMs展现了处理复杂社交场景的可能，但此前研究多局限于文本，缺乏对多模态（图像）环境下规范推理能力的评估。

Method: 通过5种不同的MLLMs对30个文本故事和30个图像故事进行规范相关问题的评估，并将其回答与人类的表现进行对比分析。

Result: MLLMs在文本环境下的规范推理表现优于图像环境。GPT-4o在两种模态下均表现最佳，Qwen-2.5VL紧随其后。然而，所有模型在面对复杂规范的推理时都表现出困难。

Conclusion: MLLMs在规范推理方面展现出潜力，尤其是GPT-4o表现最强，但模型在处理图像信息以及复杂社交规范时仍存在局限性，需进一步优化以应用于多智能体系统。

Abstract: In Multi-Agent Systems (MAS), agents are designed with social capabilities, allowing them to understand and reason about social concepts such as norms when interacting with others (e.g., inter-robot interactions). In Normative MAS (NorMAS), researchers study how norms develop, and how violations are detected and sanctioned. However, existing research in NorMAS use symbolic approaches (e.g., formal logic) for norm representation and reasoning whose application is limited to simplified environments. In contrast, Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) present promising possibilities to develop software used by robots to identify and reason about norms in a wide variety of complex social situations embodied in text and images. However, prior work on norm reasoning have been limited to text-based scenarios. This paper investigates the norm reasoning competence of five MLLMs by evaluating their ability to answer norm-related questions based on thirty text-based and thirty image-based stories, and comparing their responses against humans. Our results show that MLLMs demonstrate superior performance in norm reasoning in text than in images. GPT-4o performs the best in both modalities offering the most promise for integration with MAS, followed by the free model Qwen-2.5VL. Additionally, all models find reasoning about complex norms challenging.

</details>


### [106] [MACC: Multi-Agent Collaborative Competition for Scientific Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03780)
*Satoshi Oyama,Yuko Sakurai,Hisashi Kashima*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了 MACC 架构，旨在通过制度设计协调多个独立 AI 智能体在科学发现中的协作与竞争，以提高探索的效率和可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学发现依赖手动工作且效率低，单一人工智能体难以克服结构性局限，且现有研究忽视了独立管理智能体之间的制度性互动。

Method: 提出 MACC（多智能体协作竞争）制度架构，集成黑板式共享科学工作空间与激励机制。

Result: 构建了一个能够模拟具备激励机制、信息共享和重复性校验的自主科学探索环境。

Conclusion: MACC 为研究制度设计如何影响可扩展且可靠的多智能体科学探索提供了一个实验平台。

Abstract: Scientific discovery still relies heavily on the manual efforts of individual researchers, leading to limited exploration, redundant trials, and reduced reproducibility. Human-participant data analysis competitions generate diverse approaches, yet fluctuations in participation and the lack of independent repetitions show that parallel exploration alone is insufficient for achieving reliable scientific inquiry. As advanced AI agents based on large language models (LLMs) increasingly perform analytical tasks, relying on a single highly capable agent is unlikely to overcome these structural limitations. Recent work has begun to explore how multiple LLM-based agents can collaborate or compete in scientific workflows-a growing trend we refer to as MA4Science. However, most existing MA4Science studies assume that all agents are controlled by a single organizational entity, limiting their ability to examine how institutional mechanisms-such as incentives, information sharing, and reproducibility-shape collective exploration among independently managed agents. To address this gap, we introduce MACC (Multi-Agent Collaborative Competition), an institutional architecture that integrates a blackboard-style shared scientific workspace with incentive mechanisms designed to encourage transparency, reproducibility, and exploration efficiency. MACC provides a testbed for studying how institutional design influences scalable and reliable multi-agent scientific exploration.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [107] [Knowledge Graph and Hypergraph Transformers with Repository-Attention and Journey-Based Role Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03304)
*Mahesh Godavarti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出一种能联合处理句子和结构化数据的Transformer架构，通过分离式存储和角色传输机制实现了知识与语言的深度融合与解耦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在实现语言表示与结构化知识表示的联合训练，同时保持两者的独立分离，以解决结构化数据与文本融合时的黑盒问题。

Method: 提出一种双流架构（dual-stream），将知识图谱编码为键值库（key-value repository），并利用基于路径的语义角色传输（journey-based role transport）机制引导Transformer的注意力。

Result: 实现了语言上下文与结构化知识之间的紧密对齐，并通过交叉注意力保持了表示的显式性与可检查性。

Conclusion: 该模型通过明确分离语言和知识表示，为需要高度可解释性和结构化推理的任务提供了一种新的架构范式。

Abstract: We present a concise architecture for joint training on sentences and structured data while keeping knowledge and language representations separable. The model treats knowledge graphs and hypergraphs as structured instances with role slots and encodes them into a key-value repository that a language transformer can attend over. Attention is conditioned by journey-based role transport, which unifies edge-labeled KG traversal, hyperedge traversal, and sentence structure. We outline a dual-stream architecture, hierarchical layer groups with instance-local, neighborhood, and global mixing attention, retrieval over a separate repository, and multi-task objectives spanning masked language modeling, link prediction, and role-consistency denoising. The result is an explicit, inspectable separation between linguistic context and structured knowledge, while still enabling tight alignment through cross-attention.

</details>


### [108] [RADAR: Learning to Route with Asymmetry-aware DistAnce Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03388)
*Hang Yi,Ziwei Huang,Yining Ma,Zhiguang Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为 RADAR 的可扩展框架，通过 SVD 和 Sinkhorn 归一化增强神经求解器处理非对称车辆路径问题（AVRP）的能力，实现了更强的泛化性和性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 VRP 神经求解器多基于对称欧几里得距离假设，难以处理现实世界中的非对称距离矩阵，且直接编码非对称矩阵往往导致嵌入不紧凑及泛化性差的问题。

Method: 提出 RADAR 框架，通过 SVD 分解初始化静态的入站/出站成本特征，并使用 Sinkhorn 归一化替代标准 Softmax 以捕获注意力机制中的动态非对称交互。

Result: 在合成和真实世界的多种 VRP 基准测试中，RADAR 在分布内和分布外实例上均优于强基准模型，展现了出色的泛化能力和求解性能。

Conclusion: RADAR 成功为处理非对称 VRP 提供了强有力且具备通用性的增强方案，显著提升了现有神经求解器的实际应用价值。

Abstract: Recent neural solvers have achieved strong performance on vehicle routing problems (VRPs), yet they mainly assume symmetric Euclidean distances, restricting applicability to real-world scenarios. A core challenge is encoding the relational features in asymmetric distance matrices of VRPs. Early attempts directly encoded these matrices but often failed to produce compact embeddings and generalized poorly at scale. In this paper, we propose RADAR, a scalable neural framework that augments existing neural VRP solvers with the ability to handle asymmetric inputs. RADAR addresses asymmetry from both static and dynamic perspectives. It leverages Singular Value Decomposition (SVD) on the asymmetric distance matrix to initialize compact and generalizable embeddings that inherently encode the static asymmetry in the inbound and outbound costs of each node. To further model dynamic asymmetry in embedding interactions during encoding, it replaces the standard softmax with Sinkhorn normalization that imposes joint row and column distance awareness in attention weights. Extensive experiments on synthetic and real-world benchmarks across various VRPs show that RADAR outperforms strong baselines on both in-distribution and out-of-distribution instances, demonstrating robust generalization and superior performance in solving asymmetric VRPs.

</details>


### [109] [Heterogeneous Time Constants Improve Stability in Equilibrium Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03402)
*Yoshimasa Kubo,Suhani Pragnesh Modi,Smit Patel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究通过为平衡传播算法引入异质性神经元时间常数，提升了类脑学习算法的训练稳定性和生物拟真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的平衡传播（EP）算法使用统一的时间步长，这不符合神经元膜时间常数具有异质性（多样性）的生物学事实。

Method: 为EP中的每个神经元分配遵循生物学分布（如对数正态分布）的独立时间步长/时间常数（HTS），而非使用统一的标量。

Result: HTS 在维持与传统模型相当的任务性能的同时，显著提高了训练过程的稳定性。

Conclusion: 引入神经元特定的多样化时间常数（HTS）显著增强了平衡传播算法的稳健性与生物学合理性。

Abstract: Equilibrium propagation (EP) is a biologically plausible alternative to backpropagation for training neural networks. However, existing EP models use a uniform scalar time step dt, which corresponds biologically to a membrane time constant that is heterogeneous across neurons. Here, we introduce heterogeneous time steps (HTS) for EP by assigning neuron-specific time constants drawn from biologically motivated distributions. We show that HTS improves training stability while maintaining competitive task performance. These results suggest that incorporating heterogeneous temporal dynamics enhances both the biological realism and robustness of equilibrium propagation.

</details>


### [110] [A Short Note on a Variant of the Squint Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03409)
*Haipeng Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出并分析了 Squint 算法的一个简单变体，证明其在专家问题中具有与最新 NormalHedge 变体相当的性能界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在简化经典专家问题（Expert Problem）中的 Squint 算法，并寻求与 Freund 等人 [2026] 最新研究中 NormalHedge 变体相当的遗憾界（Regret Bound）。

Method: 对 Koolen 和 Van Erven [2015] 提出的 Squint 算法进行了简单变体设计，并同步简化了其证明过程。

Result: 该变体成功获得了与 Freund 等人 [2026] 针对 NormalHedge 变体所证出的相似遗憾界。

Conclusion: 证明了 Squint 的简化变体可以在保持算法简洁性的同时，达到与最新改进版 NormalHedge 算法相似的遗憾界。

Abstract: This short note describes a simple variant of the Squint algorithm of Koolen and Van Erven [2015] for the classic expert problem. Via an equally simple modification of their proof, we prove that this variant ensures a regret bound that resembles the one shown in a recent work by Freund et al. [2026] for a variant of the NormalHedge algorithm [Chaudhuri et al., 2009].

</details>


### [111] [Robustness of Agentic AI Systems via Adversarially-Aligned Jacobian Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04378)
*Furkan Mumcu,Yasin Yilmaz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 AAJR 正则化方法，通过仅在对抗方向限制敏感度，解决了多智能体 LLM 鲁棒训练中的不稳定问题，同时避免了全局约束带来的性能大幅下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型多智能体系统在极小极大（minimax）训练中，由于高度非线性策略导致的极端局部曲率，极易出现不稳定性。现有的全局雅可比约束过于保守，会损害模型的正常性能（鲁棒性代价过大）。

Method: 提出对抗对齐雅可比正则化（AAJR），这是一种轨迹对齐的分数方法，仅在对抗上升方向上严格控制敏感度，而非全局限制。

Result: 证明了 AAJR 在温和条件下比全局约束拥有更广的策略许可空间，减少了性能损失。同时推导出步长条件，确保了优化轨迹上的平滑度和内循环稳定性。

Conclusion: AAJR 为构建鲁棒的多智能体生态系统提供了结构性理论支持，成功解耦了极小极大算法的稳定性和全局表达能力。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) transition into autonomous multi-agent ecosystems, robust minimax training becomes essential yet remains prone to instability when highly non-linear policies induce extreme local curvature in the inner maximization. Standard remedies that enforce global Jacobian bounds are overly conservative, suppressing sensitivity in all directions and inducing a large Price of Robustness. We introduce Adversarially-Aligned Jacobian Regularization (AAJR), a trajectory-aligned approach that controls sensitivity strictly along adversarial ascent directions. We prove that AAJR yields a strictly larger admissible policy class than global constraints under mild conditions, implying a weakly smaller approximation gap and reduced nominal performance degradation. Furthermore, we derive step-size conditions under which AAJR controls effective smoothness along optimization trajectories and ensures inner-loop stability. These results provide a structural theory for agentic robustness that decouples minimax stability from global expressivity restrictions.

</details>


### [112] [[Re] FairDICE: A Gap Between Theory And Practice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03454)
*Peter Adema,Karim Galliamov,Aleksey Evstratovskiy,Ross Geurts*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究复现并修正了多目标离线强化学习算法 FairDICE。虽然原始实验因代码错误和参数不全存在误导，但修正后的算法在复杂任务中展现出潜力，尽管其对超参数较为敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多目标离线强化学习算法难以高效地在多个目标间寻找公平的平衡点。FairDICE 旨在通过改进 OptiDICE 算法，自动学习权重以实现多目标间的公平性。

Method: 通过对 FairDICE 算法进行复现研究，修正了其在连续环境中的代码错误（该错误曾导致模型退化为行为克隆），补充了缺失的超参数说明，并在更高维度的奖励和复杂环境中进行了扩展实验。

Result: 验证了原始论文的部分理论主张；发现原代码错误导致在连续环境下性能受限；证明了修正后的 FairDICE 能在大规模及高维奖励环境下运行，但高度依赖（在线）超参数调优。

Conclusion: FairDICE 是一个具有理论价值的方法，但其原始实验证据存在重大缺陷，需修正后才能在复杂场景下发挥潜力。

Abstract: Offline Reinforcement Learning (RL) is an emerging field of RL in which policies are learned solely from demonstrations. Within offline RL, some environments involve balancing multiple objectives, but existing multi-objective offline RL algorithms do not provide an efficient way to find a fair compromise. FairDICE (see arXiv:2506.08062v2) seeks to fill this gap by adapting OptiDICE (an offline RL algorithm) to automatically learn weights for multiple objectives to e.g.\ incentivise fairness among objectives. As this would be a valuable contribution, this replication study examines the replicability of claims made regarding FairDICE. We find that many theoretical claims hold, but an error in the code reduces FairDICE to standard behaviour cloning in continuous environments, and many important hyperparameters were originally underspecified. After rectifying this, we show in experiments extending the original paper that FairDICE can scale to complex environments and high-dimensional rewards, though it can be reliant on (online) hyperparameter tuning. We conclude that FairDICE is a theoretically interesting method, but the experimental justification requires significant revision.

</details>


### [113] [FedCova: Robust Federated Covariance Learning Against Noisy Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04062)
*Xiangyu Zhong,Xiaojun Yuan,Ying-Jun Angela Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 FedCova 框架，通过利用特征协方差增强模型内在鲁棒性，在不依赖外部数据的情况下解决联邦学习中的噪声标签问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中分布式的噪声标签会导致严重的局部过拟合，损害全局模型。现有方案多依赖于挑选“干净”设备或外部数据集，缺乏模型自身的内在鲁棒性。

Method: 提出了 FedCova 框架，核心在于通过互信息最大化设计了基于协方差的损失函数。利用特征子空间构建增强分类器，并基于这些子空间进行噪声标签纠正，整个过程依赖于特征协方差。

Result: 在 CIFAR-10/100 及真实噪声数据集 Clothing1M 的对称/非对称噪声和异构数据设置下，FedCova 的表现均显著优于现有的最先进方法。

Conclusion: FedCova 通过结合特征编码、分类器构建和标签纠正的统一协方差框架，有效提升了联邦学习在面对噪声标签和数据异构性时的鲁棒性，无需外部依赖。

Abstract: Noisy labels in distributed datasets induce severe local overfitting and consequently compromise the global model in federated learning (FL). Most existing solutions rely on selecting clean devices or aligning with public clean datasets, rather than endowing the model itself with robustness. In this paper, we propose FedCova, a dependency-free federated covariance learning framework that eliminates such external reliances by enhancing the model's intrinsic robustness via a new perspective on feature covariances. Specifically, FedCova encodes data into a discriminative but resilient feature space to tolerate label noise. Built on mutual information maximization, we design a novel objective for federated lossy feature encoding that relies solely on class feature covariances with an error tolerance term. Leveraging feature subspaces characterized by covariances, we construct a subspace-augmented federated classifier. FedCova unifies three key processes through the covariance: (1) training the network for feature encoding, (2) constructing a classifier directly from the learned features, and (3) correcting noisy labels based on feature subspaces. We implement FedCova across both symmetric and asymmetric noisy settings under heterogeneous data distribution. Experimental results on CIFAR-10/100 and real-world noisy dataset Clothing1M demonstrate the superior robustness of FedCova compared with the state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [114] [Minimax Optimal Strategy for Delayed Observations in Online Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03480)
*Harin Lee,Kevin Jamieson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对带随机观测延迟的强化学习问题，提出了一种基于状态增强和UCB的算法，在理论上证明了其遗憾界不仅达到最优，且适用于更广泛的结构化MDP类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究强化学习中普遍存在的“观测延迟”问题（即智能体在若干步后才能获得当前状态），旨在解决延迟带来的不确定性并寻找最优算法。

Method: 提出一种将“状态空间增强法”与“上置信界（UCB）方法”相结合的算法。同时，将该问题归纳为一类通用MDP，其转移概率由“已知部分”和“未知但有结构的部分”组成。

Result: 在表格型MDP中，该算法实现了 $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(H \sqrt{D_{\max} SAK})$ 的遗憾界。同时提供了匹配的下界证明，验证了算法的预测性与最优性。

Conclusion: 在观测延迟场景下，通过状态空间增强和UCB方法，可以达到理论上的最优遗憾界。此外，论文提出的“已知+未知结构”的状态转移分解模型为处理复杂MDP提供了通用的分析框架。

Abstract: We study reinforcement learning with delayed state observation, where the agent observes the current state after some random number of time steps. We propose an algorithm that combines the augmentation method and the upper confidence bound approach. For tabular Markov decision processes (MDPs), we derive a regret bound of $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(H \sqrt{D_{\max} SAK})$, where $S$ and $A$ are the cardinalities of the state and action spaces, $H$ is the time horizon, $K$ is the number of episodes, and $D_{\max}$ is the maximum length of the delay. We also provide a matching lower bound up to logarithmic factors, showing the optimality of our approach. Our analytical framework formulates this problem as a special case of a broader class of MDPs, where their transition dynamics decompose into a known component and an unknown but structured component. We establish general results for this abstract setting, which may be of independent interest.

</details>


### [115] [Extending Neural Operators: Robust Handling of Functions Beyond the Training Set](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03621)
*Blaine Quackenbush,Paul J. Atzberger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于再生核希尔伯特空间（RKHS）的严谨框架，用于增强神经算子处理分布外输入的能力，并能同时保证函数及导数的近似精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决神经算子在处理分布外（OOD）输入函数时缺乏严谨框架和可靠性的问题。

Method: 利用核逼近技术（kernel approximation）将神经算子扩展至 RKHS，并建立特定核选择与 Sobolev 原生空间之间的正式联系。

Result: 实验验证表明，该方法在流形上的椭圆偏微分方程（PDE）求解中表现良好，能够可靠地捕捉函数值及其导数，并分析了影响精度的关键因素。

Conclusion: 通过引入 RKHS 理论和 Sobolev 原生空间，可以建立更加鲁棒且具有数学保障的神经算子扩展框架。

Abstract: We develop a rigorous framework for extending neural operators to handle out-of-distribution input functions. We leverage kernel approximation techniques and provide theory for characterizing the input-output function spaces in terms of Reproducing Kernel Hilbert Spaces (RKHSs). We provide theorems on the requirements for reliable extensions and their predicted approximation accuracy. We also establish formal relationships between specific kernel choices and their corresponding Sobolev Native Spaces. This connection further allows the extended neural operators to reliably capture not only function values but also their derivatives. Our methods are empirically validated through the solution of elliptic partial differential equations (PDEs) involving operators on manifolds having point-cloud representations and handling geometric contributions. We report results on key factors impacting the accuracy and computational performance of the extension approaches.

</details>


### [116] [MMAI Gym for Science: Training Liquid Foundation Models for Drug Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03517)
*Maksim Kuznetsov,Zulfat Miftahutdinov,Rim Shayakhmetov,Mikolaj Mizera,Roman Schutski,Bogdan Zagribelnyy,Ivan Ilin,Nikita Bondarev,Thomas MacDougall,Mathieu Reymond,Mihir Bafna,Kaeli Kaymak-Loveless,Eugene Babin,Maxim Malkov,Mathias Lechner,Ramin Hasani,Alexander Amini,Vladimir Aladinskiy,Alex Aliper,Alex Zhavoronkov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 MMAI Gym 框架并训练了高效的 LFM 模型，证明针对分子领域优化的中小型模型在药物研发任务中优于超大规模通用模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用大语言模型（LLM）在药物研发任务中缺乏必要的科学理解，单纯增加模型规模或推理 Token 无法有效提升其在化学/生物领域的性能。

Method: 推出了 MMAI Gym for Science 框架，包含多种分子数据格式、特定任务推理和训练基准。基于此框架训练了一个高效的液态基础模型（Liquid Foundation Model, LFM）。

Result: 该模型在分子优化、ADMET 预测、逆合成等任务中达到或超越了更大型的通用/专业模型，同时保持了高效性和广泛的适用性。

Conclusion: MMAI Gym 为开发高效、专业的科学大语言模型提供了有效路径，证明了小型、目标导向的架构在复杂科学领域的潜力。

Abstract: General-purpose large language models (LLMs) that rely on in-context learning do not reliably deliver the scientific understanding and performance required for drug discovery tasks. Simply increasing model size or introducing reasoning tokens does not yield significant performance gains. To address this gap, we introduce the MMAI Gym for Science, a one-stop shop molecular data formats and modalities as well as task-specific reasoning, training, and benchmarking recipes designed to teach foundation models the 'language of molecules' in order to solve practical drug discovery problems. We use MMAI Gym to train an efficient Liquid Foundation Model (LFM) for these applications, demonstrating that smaller, purpose-trained foundation models can outperform substantially larger general-purpose or specialist models on molecular benchmarks. Across essential drug discovery tasks - including molecular optimization, ADMET property prediction, retrosynthesis, drug-target activity prediction, and functional group reasoning - the resulting model achieves near specialist-level performance and, in the majority of settings, surpasses larger models, while remaining more efficient and broadly applicable in the domain.

</details>


### [117] [A Stein Identity for q-Gaussians with Bounded Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03673)
*Sophia Sklaviadis,Thomas Moellenhoff,Andre F. T. Martins,Mario A. T. Figueiredo,Mohammad Emtiyaz Khan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文推导了适用于有界支撑 $q$-高斯分布的新 Stein 等式，提供了一种形式简洁、能降低梯度估计方差的非高斯梯度估计方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Stein 等式在处理高斯分布梯度问题时非常有效，但在非高斯分布（尤其是具有有界支撑集的分布）中应用较少，而后者在减少梯度估计方差方面具有潜力。

Method: 扩展了 Landsman 等人的研究，推导出适用于 $q$-高斯分布的新型 Bonnet 和 Price 定理，并利用伴随分布（escort distributions）简化了公式表达。

Result: 推导出的新梯度估计器在形式上与高斯分布几乎一致，易于实现；实验证明有界支撑分布能有效降低梯度估计的方差。

Conclusion: 本研究简化了 Stein 等式在非高斯分布（特别是 $q$-高斯分布）中的应用，为梯度估计提供了更广泛的数学工具。

Abstract: Stein's identity is a fundamental tool in machine learning with applications in generative models, stochastic optimization, and other problems involving gradients of expectations under Gaussian distributions. Less attention has been paid to problems with non-Gaussian expectations. Here, we consider the class of bounded-support $q$-Gaussians and derive a new Stein identity leading to gradient estimators which have nearly identical forms to the Gaussian ones, and which are similarly easy to implement. We do this by extending the previous results of Landsman, Vanduffel, and Yao (2013) to prove new Bonnet- and Price-type theorems for q-Gaussians. We also simplify their forms by using escort distributions. Our experiments show that bounded-support distributions can reduce the variance of gradient estimators, which can potentially be useful for Bayesian deep learning and sharpness-aware minimization. Overall, our work simplifies the application of Stein's identity for an important class of non-Gaussian distributions.

</details>


### [118] [Inverse Contextual Bandits without Rewards: Learning from a Non-Stationary Learner via Suffix Imitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03778)
*Yuqi Kong,Xiao Zhang,Weiran Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了如何从仅有动作的非平稳数据中反向学习策略，提出了一种简单的两阶段数据筛选机制，证明了观察者可以达到与学习者相同的收敛效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在反向上下文多臂老虎机（ICB）问题中，学习者的行为会从探索转为利用，产生非平稳数据，导致观察者难以仅凭动作准确推断奖励参数和最优策略。

Method: 提出了“两阶段后缀模仿（Two-Phase Suffix Imitation）”框架，通过舍弃初始阶段的探索数据（Burn-in phase），仅利用后续模仿阶段的数据进行经验风险最小化（ERM）。

Result: 推导出了预测决策损失界，揭示了舍弃长度与偏差-方差之间的权衡；证明了观察者能达到 $\tilde O(1/\sqrt{N})$ 的收敛率，与完全获取奖励信息的学习者渐近效率一致。

Conclusion: 被动观察者即使在缺乏奖励信息的情况下，也能从非平稳动作中恢复出最优策略，其性能极限可与主动学习者相当。

Abstract: We study the Inverse Contextual Bandit (ICB) problem, in which a learner seeks to optimize a policy while an observer, who cannot access the learner's rewards and only observes actions, aims to recover the underlying problem parameters. During the learning process, the learner's behavior naturally transitions from exploration to exploitation, resulting in non-stationary action data that poses significant challenges for the observer. To address this issue, we propose a simple and effective framework called Two-Phase Suffix Imitation. The framework discards data from an initial burn-in phase and performs empirical risk minimization using only data from a subsequent imitation phase. We derive a predictive decision loss bound that explicitly characterizes the bias-variance trade-off induced by the choice of burn-in length. Despite the severe information deficit, we show that a reward-free observer can achieve a convergence rate of $\tilde O(1/\sqrt{N})$, matching the asymptotic efficiency of a fully reward-aware learner. This result demonstrates that a passive observer can effectively uncover the optimal policy from actions alone, attaining performance comparable to that of the learner itself.

</details>


### [119] [Test-Time Meta-Adaptation with Self-Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03524)
*Zeyneb N. Kaya,Nick Rui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了 MASS 框架，通过元学习让 LLM 在推理时生成合成数据并自我更新，从而实现高效的测试时任务自适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在面对多样化领域和任务时，需要具备在推理阶段（test time）进行自我更新和自适应的能力。

Method: 引入 MASS 元学习框架。采用双层优化（bilevel optimization）：内循环（inner loop）根据自生成的合成数据进行适配更新；外循环（outer loop）通过元梯度（meta-gradients）回传下游损失，从而优化合成数据的生成质量和数据归因信号。

Result: 在数学推理实验中，MASS 证明了其能够生成特定问题的合成训练数据，并能通过针对性的自我更新显著提升模型性能。

Conclusion: MASS 能够学习生成针对特定实例的学习路径，实现高效且数据节约的测试时自适应。

Abstract: As strong general reasoners, large language models (LLMs) encounter diverse domains and tasks, where the ability to adapt and self-improve at test time is valuable. We introduce MASS, a meta-learning framework that enables LLMs to self-adapt by generating problem-specific synthetic training data and performing targeted self-updates optimized for downstream performance at inference time. We train this behavior end-to-end via bilevel optimization: an inner loop adapts on self-generated examples while an outer loop meta-learns data-attribution signals and rewards post-update task performance. The synthetic data is optimized with scalable meta-gradients, backpropagating the downstream loss through the inner updates to reward useful generations. Experiments on mathematical reasoning show that MASS learns to synthesize per-instance curricula that yield effective, data-efficient test-time adaptation.

</details>


### [120] [Fixed-Budget Constrained Best Arm Identification in Grouped Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04007)
*Raunak Mukherjee,Sharayu Moharir*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了带可行性阈值限制的分组老虎机问题，提出了 FCSR 算法，通过理论证明和实验验证了其在固定预算下识别最佳可行臂的有效性与最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究固定预算约束下的“分组多臂老虎机”问题。在这种设定中，每个臂由多个独立属性组成，只有当所有属性的均值都超过特定阈值时，该臂才被视为可行。目标是从中找出综合均值最大的可行臂。

Method: 提出了 FCSR（Feasibility Constrained Successive Rejects）算法。该算法在识别具有最大综合均值的臂时，会同步验证其所有属性是否满足阈值条件。

Result: 推导了该设定下错误概率的理论下界，并证明 FCSR 算法在指数层面上达到了除常数因子外的问题参数最优依赖。实验表明其性能优于基准算法。

Conclusion: FCSR 是解决此类分组多臂老虎机问题的有效算法，能够在维持可行性约束的同时，接近理论上的最优错误概率。

Abstract: We study fixed budget constrained best-arm identification in grouped bandits, where each arm consists of multiple independent attributes with stochastic rewards. An arm is considered feasible only if all its attributes' means are above a given threshold. The aim is to find the feasible arm with the largest overall mean. We first derive a lower bound on the error probability for any algorithm on this setting. We then propose Feasibility Constrained Successive Rejects (FCSR), a novel algorithm that identifies the best arm while ensuring feasibility. We show it attains optimal dependence on problem parameters up to constant factors in the exponent. Empirically, FCSR outperforms natural baselines while preserving feasibility guarantees.

</details>


### [121] [PTOPOFL: Privacy-Preserving Personalised Federated Learning via Persistent Homology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04323)
*Kelly L Vomo-Donfack,Adryel Hoszu,Grégory Ginot,Ian Morilla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了 PTOPOFL 框架，通过传输紧凑的模型拓扑特征而非原始梯度，在显著降低隐私泄露风险的同时，提升了非独立同分布场景下的联邦学习精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的联邦学习面临两大挑战：一是梯度共享容易导致数据重构攻击，二是数据非独立同分布（non-IID）会降低模型聚合质量。作者旨在通过拓扑学方法同时解决隐私安全与异构数据聚合问题。

Method: 框架利用持续同调（Persistent Homology, PH）提取模型的拓扑描述符（48维特征向量）代替梯度传输。服务器通过计算 PH 图之间的 Wasserstein 距离进行客户端聚类，并实施拓扑加权的个性化模型聚合。

Result: 在医疗和病理基准测试中，PTOPOFL 分别达到了 0.841 和 0.910 的 AUC，性能优于 FedAvg 等主流框架。同时，其数据重构风险较梯度共享降低了 4.5 倍，且在强凸损失函数下具有线性收敛性。

Conclusion: PTOPOFL 证明了拓扑特征可以作为梯度信息的鲁棒替代方案，不仅增强了联邦学习的隐私防御能力，还通过拓扑相似度聚类有效缓解了非独立同分布（non-IID）带来的性能退化。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) faces two structural tensions: gradient sharing enables data-reconstruction attacks, while non-IID client distributions degrade aggregation quality. We introduce PTOPOFL, a framework that addresses both challenges simultaneously by replacing gradient communication with topological descriptors derived from persistent homology (PH). Clients transmit only 48-dimensional PH feature vectors-compact shape summaries whose many-to-one structure makes inversion provably ill-posed-rather than model gradients. The server performs topology-guided personalised aggregation: clients are clustered by Wasserstein similarity between their PH diagrams, intra-cluster models are topology-weighted,and clusters are blended with a global consensus. We prove an information-contraction theorem showing that PH descriptors leak strictly less mutual information per sample than gradients under strongly convex loss functions, and we establish linear convergence of the Wasserstein-weighted aggregation scheme with an error floor strictly smaller than FedAvg. Evaluated against FedAvg, FedProx, SCAFFOLD, and pFedMe on a non-IID healthcare scenario (8 hospitals, 2 adversarial) and a pathological benchmark (10 clients), PTOPOFL achieves AUC 0.841 and 0.910 respectively-the highest in both settings-while reducing reconstruction risk by a factor of 4.5 relative to gradient sharing. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/MorillaLab/TopoFederatedL and data at https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.18827595.

</details>


### [122] [Directional Neural Collapse Explains Few-Shot Transfer in Self-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03530)
*Achleshwar Luthra,Yash Salunkhe,Tomer Galanti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出“定向CDNV”这一概念，揭示了SSL表示在少样本迁移中表现优异且多任务干扰小的原因在于沿分类方向的方差塌陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自监督表示在少样本迁移和多任务处理上表现优异，但缺乏一个统一的几何指标来解释这种广泛的适用性和低干扰性。

Method: 定义了定向CDNV（决定轴方差），并证明了其在多类分类泛化界限中的作用，同时研究了其与多任务几何（正交性）之间的联系。

Result: 1. 理论上推导出了以定向CDNV为核心的非渐近泛化界限；2. 证明了小的定向CDNV会促使不同任务的决定轴趋于正交；3. 实验显示SSL预训练过程中定向CDNV显著下降，且界限能准确追踪少样本误差。

Conclusion: 定向CDNV可以作为自监督学习中下游任务泛化性能和多任务正交性的核心几何衡量指标。

Abstract: Frozen self-supervised representations often transfer well with only a few labels across many semantic tasks. We argue that a single geometric quantity, \emph{directional} CDNV (decision-axis variance), sits at the core of two favorable behaviors: strong few-shot transfer within a task, and low interference across many tasks. We show that both emerge when variability \emph{along} class-separating directions is small. First, we prove sharp non-asymptotic multiclass generalization bounds for downstream classification whose leading term is the directional CDNV. The bounds include finite-shot corrections that cleanly separate intrinsic decision-axis variability from centroid-estimation error. Second, we link decision-axis collapse to multitask geometry: for independent balanced labelings, small directional CDNV across tasks forces the corresponding decision axes to be nearly orthogonal, helping a single representation support many tasks with minimal interference. Empirically, across SSL objectives, directional CDNV collapses during pretraining even when classical CDNV remains large, and our bounds closely track few-shot error at practical shot sizes. Additionally, on synthetic multitask data, we verify that SSL learns representations whose induced decision axes are nearly orthogonal. The code and project page of the paper are available at [\href{https://dlfundamentals.github.io/directional-neural-collapse/}{project page}].

</details>


### [123] [Role-Aware Conditional Inference for Spatiotemporal Ecosystem Carbon Flux Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03531)
*Yiming Sun,Runlong Yu,Rongchao Dong,Shuo Chen,Licheng Liu,Youmi Oh,Qianlai Zhuang,Yiqun Xie,Xiaowei Jia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 RACI 框架，通过分离环境变量的快慢角色并引入空间检索增强，解决了陆地碳通量预测中因时空异质性导致的泛化难题。


<details>
